# Phase 4.8 closing megaudit (4.8.A → L + W5-124)

You are reviewing the cumulative Phase 4.8 (structured references +
tables) arc. The arc is feature-complete. Your job is to find
integration-level issues that single-phase audits missed — the kind
of issue that only surfaces when multiple sub-phases interact.

This pattern caught a HIGH BLOCKER at the prior 4.7.O megaudit
(commit `34fb2d46311`). Treat it as load-bearing — go deeper than
"each sub-phase reviewed individually."

## Context

- Branch: `feat/quantbook-engine`. HEAD `7ec316402e8`.
- Design doc: `docs/architecture/2026-05-14-structured-references-and-tables.md`
  (765 lines, including § 14 status table + § 17 Codex pass-1 closure
  summary).
- Pre-arc HEAD: `9e5085a5405` (the prior session's Phase 4.8.I close).
- 2384 workspace tests, 0 failed, 1 ignored, all 7 gates green.

## Commits in scope (chronological)

```
ca4faa62dad  W5-121 / 4.8.I.2  rename_column + formula-text rewrite          (+11 tests)
666af927b3a  W5-122 / 4.8.J    resize_table + Op::ResizeTable                (+13 tests)
1e713cffbc7  W5-123 / 4.8.L    schema v5 → v6 + TableTable persistence       (+12 tests)
7ec316402e8  W5-124 / 4.8.J.2  uniform spill-anchor check (create+resize)    (+4 tests)
```

Two fmt-only cleanup commits (`749562bf41f`, `0bb7ef8aa9a`) precede
these but carry no semantic change.

## Areas to probe (in priority order)

### A. Cross-sub-phase invariants

These are the "only surfaces when N≥2 sub-phases meet" issues.

1. **Op log replay ordering invariants.** A producer session that
   does `create_table → rename_column → resize_table` emits a
   specific sequence. When replayed against a fresh workbook, does
   the order survive? Specifically: does `apply_rename_column`
   accept a column id that `apply_create_table` allocated, AND does
   the post-rename `apply_resize_table` see the renamed column in
   its trailing-column check?

2. **`TableTable::generation` accounting.** Every mutation must
   bump it (or the plan cache stays warm with stale `is_aggregate`
   resolutions). Check: create / drop / rename_table / rename_column
   / resize all bump it. Look for paths that mutate via `get_mut`
   without an explicit `bump_generation()` call. Are there any?

3. **`next_column_id` allocator preservation.** W5-123 added
   auto-bump in `TableTable::insert`. Verify: producer creates
   `Sales[Qty,Price]` (ids 0, 1) → resize_table appends `Discount`
   (id 2) → save → load → resize_table appends `Tax`. Does Tax get
   id 3 (correct) or 0 (collision)?

4. **`.qbook` persistence × runtime mutation interaction.** Save a
   workbook with a table. Reload. Now `rename_column` on the
   reloaded table. Does the rewrite walk over the reloaded formula
   text and produce identical output to the pre-save version?

5. **Spill-anchor uniform check (W5-124) — completeness.** The
   producer-side check fires at `create_table` and `resize_table`.
   But: what about `Workbook::tables_mut().insert(...)` (the
   loader's direct insertion path)? Does the loader at 4.8.L bypass
   the spill-anchor check? Is that intentional (load-time data is
   trusted) or a gap?

### B. Formula-text rewrite correctness (4.8.I + 4.8.I.2)

6. **String literals containing column names.** A formula like
   `="The Qty column"` — does `rename_column("Sales", "Qty",
   "Quantity")` accidentally rewrite the string literal? Walk
   through `rewrite_column_ref` mentally. (Same question for
   `rename_table`.)

7. **Specials-with-column-named-#Foo collision.** A table with a
   column literally named `#Headers` (escaped in source as
   `'#Headers`). Does `rename_column("Sales", "#Headers", "Hdrs")`
   correctly find the right column? Does the AST walker
   distinguish `TableSpecItem::Column("#Headers")` from
   `TableSpecItem::Special(SpecialItem::Headers)`?

8. **Plan-cache invalidation drift.** Rename and resize both do
   `plan_cache.clear()`. Is there a faster path (e.g.
   `plan_cache.clear_for_table(name)`) that should exist? Are there
   formulas NOT referencing the renamed/resized table that get
   needlessly re-bound on next eval?

### C. resize_table edge cases (4.8.J)

9. **Same-canonical no-op semantics — does it match rename?**
   `rename_column` returns `Ok(0)` for same-canonical, emits no op.
   Does `resize_table` do anything analogous when called with
   `new_rows == old_rows && new_cols == old_cols && added.is_empty()
   && removed.is_empty()`? Or does it always emit an op even for
   a no-op resize? Should it?

10. **Resize to zero data rows.** A table with `has_header=true,
    rows=3` (header + 2 data rows). Resize to `rows=1` (header only,
    no data). Does `SUM(Sales[Qty])` correctly surface
    `BindError::StructuredRefDegenerateRange` on next bind?

11. **Resize with column id pollution.** Producer adds column,
    then immediately drops it via two `resize_table` calls. The
    added column burned a `next_column_id`. Is that intentional
    or wasteful? (Probably intentional — match Postgres SERIAL
    semantics — but worth confirming the design intent.)

### D. Persistence (4.8.L)

12. **Round-trip determinism.** Save → load → save → diff the
    two `workbook.toml` files. Are they byte-identical? Test the
    sort orders (`tables_section` sorts by canonical name; do
    column orders within a table preserve insertion order or get
    sorted?).

13. **`MalformedTable` coverage.** The loader validates 3 things:
    sheet exists, non-zero dims, columns.len matches cols. What
    about: a column with empty `name` or empty `display`? A
    `totals_function: Some(Custom)` (does anything else need to be
    valid)? `top_row/top_col + rows/cols` overflowing `RowId/ColId`?

14. **Forward-compat sketch.** A v6 file with a future v7-only
    field in `TableColumnEntry` (e.g. `style_id: u32`) — does
    `deny_unknown_fields` correctly reject it? Important because
    silent acceptance of unknown fields here would cause the same
    class of bug that the v2 schema bump explicitly fixed (M9).

### E. Backwards compat

15. **Pre-existing fixtures.** Are there any test fixtures of v1-v5
    `.qbook` files in the repo (`tests/fixtures/`, `.references/`)?
    Do they all still load on v6 without the workbook becoming
    semantically different (e.g. an empty `TableTable` is fine, but
    a phantom format entry from v4 shouldn't appear)?

### F. Test coverage gaps

16. List any obvious test gaps. Specifically: every public method
    added in W5-121/W5-122/W5-123/W5-124 — is there at least one
    direct test? At least one round-trip test through op log?

## Output

Triage your findings as:

- **HIGH** = ship blocker; the engine can produce an inconsistent
  state or a crash from normal use.
- **MEDIUM** = correctness or robustness issue; user-visible but
  recoverable.
- **LOW** = polish; doc drift, dead code, naming, etc.

Each finding should cite: file path, line range, one-sentence
description of what's wrong, one-sentence proposed fix.

If a finding is uncertain (you'd need to read more), flag as
"NEEDS-INVESTIGATION" and describe what to check.

Be specific — generic advice like "consider edge cases" is not
useful. Concrete findings ("at `workbook_runtime.rs:1820`, when
`removed_columns.len() == old_cols`, the trailing_start cast to
usize underflows") are what we need.

## Hard rules

- Do NOT propose fixes that introduce fallbacks (CLAUDE.md hard
  rule: errors must be visible).
- Do NOT propose breaking the schema-version range check.
- Do NOT propose lowering test count.

End with a one-line verdict: **SHIP-READY** or **NOT SHIP-READY**.

./docs/architecture/2026-05-14-structured-references-and-tables.md:158:5. **Tables block ALL spill anchors inside their footprint** — REVISED post-Codex (HIGH-3). Original draft allowed within-table spills if they fit; Excel canon DOES NOT allow array formulas inside Tables. `=SEQUENCE(3)` typed into ANY cell inside a table footprint produces `#SPILL!` at the anchor (even if the resulting array would fit). Scalar formulas inside tables remain allowed. Validation runs at THREE entry points (W5-124): (a) `write_spill` consults `Workbook::table_at(anchor)` before registering — blocks the "type array formula INSIDE existing table" path; (b) `create_table` walks the proposed footprint checking `Workbook::spill_anchor_at` — blocks the "create table OVER existing anchor" path; (c) `resize_table` walks NEWLY claimed cells with the same check — blocks the "grow table TO COVER existing anchor" path. Replay arms mirror (b) + (c).
./docs/architecture/2026-05-14-structured-references-and-tables.md:168:Mirrors `name_to_formulas` (existing in `CalcgraphSession`). Every formula's `extract_and_register_deps` pass discovers `StructuredRef` references and registers them in `table_to_formulas: HashMap<Arc<str>, HashSet<NodeId>>`. The runtime fires `on_table_create / on_table_rename / on_column_rename / on_table_resize / on_table_drop` hooks against this index when table metadata mutates. These hooks dirty the indexed formulas + transitively propagate dirty downstream (matching the W5-91 / `on_set_name` BFS fanout pattern). Without this index, `resize_table` growth would NOT dirty formulas reading the new rows.
./docs/architecture/2026-05-14-structured-references-and-tables.md:371:   - `BindError::StructuredRefDegenerateRange(Arc<str>)` (e.g., `#Data` on a header-only table)
./docs/architecture/2026-05-14-structured-references-and-tables.md:415:| `StructuredRefDegenerateRange` | `Value::Error(#CALC!)` (consistent with 4.7) |
./docs/architecture/2026-05-14-structured-references-and-tables.md:421:The original draft proposed a `table_gen: u64` field in `PlanCacheKey`. Per HIGH-1 closure (§ 12.2 — formula text is rewritten on rename), `table_gen` is no longer needed for renames. It IS needed for non-rename mutations: `resize_table` (range changes; cached plans hold the OLD resolved range), `column_rename` (column index changes), `drop_table` (cached plans hold a now-invalid range). Add `table_gen` for these cases:

./docs/audits/2026-05-13-phase-4.6-design-codex-review.txt:23:The current qbook format uses `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` and its documented policy says any future additive field requires a schema bump. Adding `scope` to `NamedEntry` or `NamedTargetWire` while keeping schema v4 means older v4 readers will treat the new file as "supported schema 4" and then fail during TOML deserialization with an unknown field, not with `UnsupportedSchema`. That is exactly the forward-compat hygiene the current schema policy was designed to avoid.
./docs/audits/2026-05-13-phase-4.6-design-codex-review.txt:59:MEDIUM-3: Sheet-scoped names need a cache-generation and lookup contract.
./docs/audits/2026-05-13-phase-4.6-design-codex-review.txt:61:Option A, per-sheet `NameTable`, is workable, but the doc only discusses workbook `name_gen` for PlanCache invalidation. A sheet-scoped-name mutation can change the meaning of formulas on that sheet while leaving `Workbook::names().generation()` unchanged. That creates stale cached plans unless the cache key includes a combined generation, such as `(workbook_name_gen, sheet_name_gen_for_owning_sheet, sheet_registry_gen)`, or the runtime flushes/increments a global semantic generation on any name/sheet mutation.
./docs/audits/2026-05-13-phase-4.6-design-codex-review.txt:123:A -> B -> C -> D is close, but the real first deliverable should be "sheet registry API and canonicalizer" before parser work. Parser context, rename collision checks, printer inverse lookup, qbook load validation, and IDE diagnostics all depend on that. Sheet-scoped names can land after rename only if the rename design already accounts for per-sheet name tables and cache generations.
./docs/audits/2026-05-13-phase-4.6-design-codex-review.txt:133:Schema bump policy: HIGH. New `scope` field requires qbook schema v5 under the current `deny_unknown_fields` policy.
./docs/audits/2026-05-13-phase-4.6-design-codex-review.txt:154:6. Add PlanCache key policy for `sheet_gen` or global `semantic_gen`, plus sheet-scoped-name generation.

./docs/audits/2026-05-14-phase-4.6-closing-megaudit-sonnet.md:184:**I.3 — `deny_unknown_fields` on `NamedEntry`:** FAIL — LOW  
./docs/audits/2026-05-14-phase-4.6-closing-megaudit-sonnet.md:185:`NamedEntry` at `qbook_format.rs:363–377` does NOT have `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]`, while `WorkbookEnvelope` (line 248) and `SheetEnvelope` (line 301) both do. This means a v6+ entry with an additional field in a `NamedEntry` object would be silently ignored rather than loudly rejected. The existing v1-v4 / v5 compatibility is unaffected, but the "strict forward-compat" invariant (established in Phase 2A.8 megaudit) is violated for this struct. Severity: LOW (only matters if a future schema version adds a `NamedEntry` field without bumping the version gate).
./docs/audits/2026-05-14-phase-4.6-closing-megaudit-sonnet.md:197:`qbook_format.rs:1222–1228` rejects `schema_version > 5` (or, from a v4 reader's perspective, `> 4`). The version gate is the primary safety net. The secondary safety (`serde(default)` + `deny_unknown_fields` — not present on `NamedEntry`) is a gap (I.3 above) but not a regression from v4.

./docs/audits/2026-05-14-phase-4.7-design-codex-review.txt:546:The first draft proposed extending v4 in place. Codex flagged that the workbook envelope uses `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` per Phase 2A.8 policy: any new field requires a schema bump. Adding `scope` to `NamedEntry` in-place at v4 means older v4 readers fail with serde-unknown-field deserialize errors rather than the documented `UnsupportedSchema` path.
./docs/audits/2026-05-14-phase-4.7-design-codex-review.txt:1097:Spill-anchor changes don't affect plan binding (the formula text + sheet + name generation determine the plan). No new cache-invalidation surface; the existing W5-91/W5-93 invalidation rules suffice.

./docs/audits/2026-05-14-phase-4.6-closing-megaudit-codex.txt:507:The first draft proposed extending v4 in place. Codex flagged that the workbook envelope uses `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` per Phase 2A.8 policy: any new field requires a schema bump. Adding `scope` to `NamedEntry` in-place at v4 means older v4 readers fail with serde-unknown-field deserialize errors rather than the documented `UnsupportedSchema` path.

     1	//! **W5-110 (Phase 4.8.A):** table metadata storage.
     2	//!
     3	//! Mirrors the [`crate::NameTable`] pattern (Phase 2A.1). Tables are
     4	//! workbook-level entities: a named, rectangular range with column
     5	//! metadata + header/totals row flags. Structured references like
     6	//! `Sales[Qty]` resolve through this table at bind time.
     7	//!
     8	//! This module is **data model only** for 4.8.A. Mutation API (create /
     9	//! rename / resize / drop) lands in 4.8.H + 4.8.I + 4.8.J via the
    10	//! `WorkbookRuntime` layer with op-log emission. Direct calls into
    11	//! [`TableTable`]'s mutation methods are reserved for the qbook loader
    12	//! and tests; product code uses the runtime API.
    13	//!
    14	//! See `docs/architecture/2026-05-14-structured-references-and-tables.md`
    15	//! § 4 for the data-model design.
    16	
    17	use std::collections::HashMap;
    18	use std::sync::Arc;
    19	
    20	use ql_types::{Address, ColId, Range, RowId, SheetId};
    21	
    22	/// **W5-110 (Phase 4.8.A):** the per-column metadata inside a [`TableMetadata`].
    23	///
    24	/// The `id` field is a stable per-column identifier monotonically allocated
    25	/// from the workbook-level counter (`TableTable::next_column_id`, private).
    26	/// Stable across renames within the table; never reused. Future phases (4.10
    27	/// calculated columns, Phase 5 column move) consume `id` to disambiguate
    28	/// columns even when names collide ephemerally.
    29	///
    30	/// `name` is the lowercase canonical form for case-insensitive lookup;
    31	/// `display` preserves the original case for printer round-trip.
    32	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    33	pub struct TableColumn {
    34	    /// Stable id, monotonically allocated. NOT the index in
    35	    /// [`TableMetadata::columns`] — removing a column does NOT renumber
    36	    /// subsequent ids.
    37	    pub id: u32,
    38	    /// Lowercase canonical name.
    39	    pub name: Arc<str>,
    40	    /// Case-preserving display name.
    41	    pub display: Arc<str>,
    42	    /// Phase 4.10 will auto-populate the totals row when this is `Some`;
    43	    /// 4.8 just stores the metadata.
    44	    pub totals_function: Option<TotalsFunction>,
    45	}
    46	
    47	/// **W5-110 (Phase 4.8.A):** per-column totals-row function metadata.
    48	///
    49	/// Phase 4.8 stores this; Phase 4.10 will auto-populate the totals row's
    50	/// formula text from it. `Custom` indicates a user-typed formula in the
    51	/// totals row that doesn't match any of the canonical Excel totals
    52	/// functions.
    53	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    54	pub enum TotalsFunction {
    55	    None,
    56	    Average,
    57	    Count,
    58	    CountNums,
    59	    Max,
    60	    Min,
    61	    StdDev,
    62	    Sum,
    63	    Variance,
    64	    Custom,
    65	}
    66	
    67	/// **W5-110 (Phase 4.8.A):** workbook-level table metadata.
    68	///
    69	/// A table is a named rectangular range with column metadata. Structured
    70	/// references `Sales[Qty]` resolve through this struct at bind time.
    71	///
    72	/// **Coordinate model:** `top_row` / `top_col` is the top-left cell;
    73	/// `rows × cols` covers the FULL footprint including header + totals
    74	/// rows. `has_header` (true) means row `top_row` is the header row;
    75	/// `has_totals` (true) means row `top_row + rows - 1` is the totals row.
    76	///
    77	/// **Range helpers** ([`Self::header_range`], [`Self::totals_range`],
    78	/// [`Self::data_range`], [`Self::all_range`], [`Self::column_data_range`])
    79	/// compute concrete `Range` values from the metadata; they are pure
    80	/// functions over `&self` with no internal caching.
    81	///
    82	/// See the design doc § 4.1 for invariants + § 4.3 for the workbook-level
    83	/// invariants (overlap rules, namespace sharing with `NameTable`, etc.).
    84	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    85	pub struct TableMetadata {
    86	    /// Canonical name (uppercase). Mirrors `NameTable`'s canonicalization.
    87	    pub name: Arc<str>,
    88	    /// Display name (case-preserving). Defaults to `name` if no
    89	    /// case-preserving form was supplied at create time.
    90	    pub display_name: Arc<str>,
    91	    /// Anchor sheet.
    92	    pub sheet: SheetId,
    93	    /// Top-left row of the table's full footprint.
    94	    pub top_row: RowId,
    95	    /// Top-left column.
    96	    pub top_col: ColId,
    97	    /// Total rows including header + totals.
    98	    pub rows: u32,
    99	    /// Total columns.
   100	    pub cols: u32,
   101	    /// `true` iff `top_row` is a header row.
   102	    pub has_header: bool,
   103	    /// `true` iff `top_row + rows - 1` is a totals row.
   104	    pub has_totals: bool,
   105	    /// Ordered column roster. Length == `cols`. Lookup by canonical
   106	    /// (lowercase) name; case-preserving display in each entry.
   107	    pub columns: Vec<TableColumn>,
   108	}
   109	
   110	impl TableMetadata {
   111	    /// The header row's range if `has_header`, else `None`. Single-row
   112	    /// `Range` covering all columns.
   113	    pub fn header_range(&self) -> Option<Range> {
   114	        if !self.has_header {
   115	            return None;
   116	        }
   117	        Some(Range::new(
   118	            self.sheet,
   119	            self.top_row,
   120	            self.top_col,
   121	            self.top_row,
   122	            self.top_col + self.cols - 1,
   123	        ))
   124	    }
   125	
   126	    /// The totals row's range if `has_totals`, else `None`.
   127	    pub fn totals_range(&self) -> Option<Range> {
   128	        if !self.has_totals {
   129	            return None;
   130	        }
   131	        let r = self.top_row + self.rows - 1;
   132	        Some(Range::new(
   133	            self.sheet,
   134	            r,
   135	            self.top_col,
   136	            r,
   137	            self.top_col + self.cols - 1,
   138	        ))
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    /// The data range — rows excluding header + totals. Returns `None`
   142	    /// for header-only / totals-only / header+totals-only tables where
   143	    /// no data rows exist (`ql_types::Range` is inclusive and can't
   144	    /// represent zero rows; see design § 16 q4 — binder maps `None`
   145	    /// here to `BindError::StructuredRefDegenerateRange`).
   146	    pub fn data_range(&self) -> Option<Range> {
   147	        let mut first_data = self.top_row;
   148	        let mut last_data = self.top_row + self.rows - 1;
   149	        if self.has_header {
   150	            first_data += 1;
   151	        }
   152	        if self.has_totals {
   153	            // last_data is the totals row; data ends one above it.
   154	            if last_data == 0 {
   155	                return None;
   156	            }
   157	            last_data -= 1;
   158	        }
   159	        if first_data > last_data {
   160	            return None;
   161	        }
   162	        Some(Range::new(
   163	            self.sheet,
   164	            first_data,
   165	            self.top_col,
   166	            last_data,
   167	            self.top_col + self.cols - 1,
   168	        ))
   169	    }
   170	
   171	    /// The full table footprint, including header + totals if present.
   172	    pub fn all_range(&self) -> Range {
   173	        Range::new(
   174	            self.sheet,
   175	            self.top_row,
   176	            self.top_col,
   177	            self.top_row + self.rows - 1,
   178	            self.top_col + self.cols - 1,
   179	        )
   180	    }
   181	
   182	    /// The data range for a single column, indexed by column position
   183	    /// (0..self.cols). `None` if `col_idx` out of range OR no data rows.
   184	    pub fn column_data_range(&self, col_idx: u32) -> Option<Range> {
   185	        if col_idx >= self.cols {
   186	            return None;
   187	        }
   188	        let data = self.data_range()?;
   189	        let col = self.top_col + col_idx;
   190	        Some(Range::new(
   191	            self.sheet,
   192	            data.start_row,
   193	            col,
   194	            data.end_row,
   195	            col,
   196	        ))
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    /// Look up a column by case-insensitive name. Returns `(col_idx,
   200	    /// &TableColumn)` on hit. The canonical (lowercase) name lives in
   201	    /// `TableColumn::name`; this method uppercases the query first so
   202	    /// callers don't need to pre-canonicalize.
   203	    pub fn lookup_column(&self, name: &str) -> Option<(u32, &TableColumn)> {
   204	        let lower = name.to_ascii_lowercase();
   205	        self.columns
   206	            .iter()
   207	            .enumerate()
   208	            .find(|(_, c)| c.name.as_ref() == lower.as_str())
   209	            .map(|(i, c)| (i as u32, c))
   210	    }
   211	
   212	    /// Does `addr` fall inside this table's full footprint (sheet +
   213	    /// bounding rectangle)? Used by the `Workbook::table_at` reverse lookup.
   214	    pub fn contains(&self, addr: Address) -> bool {
   215	        addr.sheet == self.sheet
   216	            && addr.row >= self.top_row
   217	            && addr.row < self.top_row + self.rows
   218	            && addr.col >= self.top_col
   219	            && addr.col < self.top_col + self.cols
   220	    }
   221	}
   222	
   223	/// **W5-110 (Phase 4.8.A):** workbook-level table registry.
   224	///
   225	/// Mirrors [`crate::NameTable`]: HashMap-backed storage keyed by
   226	/// uppercase-canonical name + generation counter for plan-cache
   227	/// invalidation. The `next_column_id` allocator hands out monotonic
   228	/// per-column ids across the whole workbook (one counter, all tables
   229	/// share); future phases (4.10 calculated columns, Phase 5 column move)
   230	/// consume these ids.
   231	///
   232	/// **No mutation API in 4.8.A.** Direct `tables.insert` from the qbook
   233	/// loader is fine; product code mutates via `WorkbookRuntime::create_table`
   234	/// etc. (4.8.H+) which emit op-log entries.
   235	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
   236	pub struct TableTable {
   237	    tables: HashMap<Arc<str>, TableMetadata>,
   238	    /// Monotonic counter bumped on every successful mutation. Plan-cache
   239	    /// invalidation keys can include this so column renames / resizes
   240	    /// invalidate cached `ExprPlan::StructuredRef` entries. See design
   241	    /// § 7.5.
   242	    generation: u64,
   243	    /// Monotonic per-column-id allocator. Shared across all tables.
   244	    /// Allocated at column-create time; never reused.
   245	    next_column_id: u32,
   246	}
   247	
   248	impl TableTable {
   249	    pub fn new() -> Self {
   250	        Self::default()
   251	    }
   252	
   253	    /// Bind-plan cache invalidation counter (see [`NameTable::generation`]
   254	    /// for the pattern). Bumped on every successful mutation.
   255	    pub fn generation(&self) -> u64 {
   256	        self.generation
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    /// Case-insensitive lookup. Uppercases the query.
   260	    pub fn lookup(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&TableMetadata> {
   261	        let upper = name.to_ascii_uppercase();
   262	        self.tables.get(upper.as_str())
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    /// Reverse lookup: which table contains the given cell, if any?
   266	    /// O(num_tables) scan; tables-per-workbook is small (typically < 100).
   267	    ///
   268	    /// Used by:
   269	    /// - the binder for `[@Col]` resolution (which table is the formula in?).
   270	    /// - `create_table` / `resize_table` validation (does the proposed
   271	    ///   footprint overlap another table?).
   272	    pub fn table_at(&self, addr: Address) -> Option<&TableMetadata> {
   273	        self.tables.values().find(|t| t.contains(addr))
   274	    }
   275	
   276	    /// All table entries; HashMap-arbitrary order.
   277	    pub fn iter(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&Arc<str>, &TableMetadata)> + '_ {
   278	        self.tables.iter()
   279	    }

   541	/// **W5-119 (Phase 4.8.I):** replay a `RenameTable`. Validates source
   542	/// exists, target name is available (TableTable + NameTable shared
   543	/// namespace), then re-keys the entry. Note: formula text rewrites
   544	/// arrive as accompanying `Op::PutFormula` ops; this arm doesn't
   545	/// touch formula cells.
   546	fn apply_rename_table(
   547	    workbook: &mut Workbook,
   548	    index: usize,
   549	    old_name: &str,
   550	    new_name: &str,
   551	) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
   552	    let old_canonical = old_name.to_ascii_uppercase();
   553	    let new_canonical: std::sync::Arc<str> =
   554	        std::sync::Arc::from(new_name.to_ascii_uppercase().as_str());
   555	
   556	    // Verify source.
   557	    if workbook.tables().lookup(&old_canonical).is_none() {
   558	        return Err(ReplayError::TableNotFound {
   559	            index,
   560	            name: old_name.to_owned(),
   561	        });
   562	    }
   563	    // Verify target availability (skip the no-op same-name case).
   564	    if !old_canonical.eq_ignore_ascii_case(new_name) {
   565	        if workbook.tables().lookup(&new_canonical).is_some() {
   566	            return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   567	                index,
   568	                name: new_name.to_owned(),
   569	                reason: "table with this canonical name already exists (rename target)",
   570	            });
   571	        }
   572	        if workbook.names().lookup_ci(&new_canonical).is_some() {
   573	            return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   574	                index,
   575	                name: new_name.to_owned(),
   576	                reason: "defined-name with this canonical name already exists (rename target)",
   577	            });
   578	        }
   579	    }
   580	    // Take the entry out, mutate name + display, reinsert under new key.
   581	    let mut meta = workbook
   582	        .tables_mut()
   583	        .remove(&old_canonical)
   584	        .expect("verified above");
   585	    meta.name = std::sync::Arc::clone(&new_canonical);
   586	    meta.display_name = std::sync::Arc::from(new_name);
   587	    workbook.tables_mut().insert(new_canonical, meta);
   588	    Ok(())
   589	}
   590	
   591	/// **W5-121 (Phase 4.8.I.2):** replay a `RenameColumn`. Validates the
   592	/// target table exists, the source column exists (case-insensitive), and
   593	/// the target column name is available within the table. Mutates the
   594	/// matching `TableColumn`'s `name` (lowercase canonical) and `display`
   595	/// (case-preserving). Bumps the `TableTable` generation so plan caches
   596	/// invalidate. Same-canonical column renames are rejected — the producer
   597	/// side treats those as a no-op and doesn't emit the op, so receiving
   598	/// one indicates log divergence.
   599	fn apply_rename_column(
   600	    workbook: &mut ql_storage::Workbook,
   601	    index: usize,
   602	    table: &str,
   603	    old_name: &str,
   604	    new_name: &str,
   605	) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
   606	    let table_canonical = table.to_ascii_uppercase();
   607	    let meta = workbook
   608	        .tables_mut()
   609	        .get_mut(&table_canonical)
   610	        .ok_or_else(|| ReplayError::TableNotFound {
   611	            index,
   612	            name: table.to_owned(),
   613	        })?;
   614	    let (col_idx, _) =
   615	        meta.lookup_column(old_name)
   616	            .ok_or_else(|| ReplayError::TableColumnNotFound {
   617	                index,
   618	                table: table.to_owned(),
   619	                column: old_name.to_owned(),
   620	            })?;
   621	    if new_name.is_empty() {
   622	        return Err(ReplayError::TableColumnRejected {
   623	            index,
   624	            table: table.to_owned(),
   625	            column: new_name.to_owned(),
   626	            reason: "column name cannot be empty",
   627	        });
   628	    }
   629	    let old_lower = old_name.to_ascii_lowercase();
   630	    let new_lower = new_name.to_ascii_lowercase();
   631	    if old_lower == new_lower {
   632	        return Err(ReplayError::TableColumnRejected {
   633	            index,
   634	            table: table.to_owned(),
   635	            column: new_name.to_owned(),
   636	            reason: "rename to same canonical column name (producer should not emit)",
   637	        });
   638	    }
   639	    if meta.lookup_column(new_name).is_some() {
   640	        return Err(ReplayError::TableColumnRejected {
   641	            index,
   642	            table: table.to_owned(),
   643	            column: new_name.to_owned(),
   644	            reason: "column with this canonical name already exists (rename target)",
   645	        });
   646	    }
   647	    meta.columns[col_idx as usize].name = std::sync::Arc::from(new_lower.as_str());
   648	    meta.columns[col_idx as usize].display = std::sync::Arc::from(new_name);
   649	    workbook.tables_mut().bump_generation();
   650	    Ok(())
   651	}
   652	
   653	/// **W5-122 (Phase 4.8.J):** replay a `ResizeTable`. Validates the
   654	/// target table exists, the arithmetic invariant
   655	/// (`new_cols == old_cols + added.len() - removed.len()`),
   656	/// `removed_columns` exactly match the current trailing columns
   657	/// (case-insensitive on display), the final column roster has unique
   658	/// canonical names, and the new footprint cells outside the old
   659	/// footprint don't overlap other tables. Mutates the table metadata
   660	/// in place (rows/cols + columns vec). Bumps `TableTable` generation.
   661	fn apply_resize_table(
   662	    workbook: &mut ql_storage::Workbook,
   663	    index: usize,
   664	    name: &str,
   665	    new_rows: u32,
   666	    new_cols: u32,
   667	    added_columns: &[String],
   668	    removed_columns: &[String],
   669	) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
   670	    use ql_storage::TableColumn;
   671	    let canonical = name.to_ascii_uppercase();
   672	    // Snapshot the immutable bits we need to validate before mutating.
   673	    let (sheet, top_row, top_col, old_rows, old_cols, old_displays) = {
   674	        let meta =
   675	            workbook
   676	                .tables()
   677	                .lookup(&canonical)
   678	                .ok_or_else(|| ReplayError::TableNotFound {
   679	                    index,
   680	                    name: name.to_owned(),
   681	                })?;
   682	        (
   683	            meta.sheet,
   684	            meta.top_row,
   685	            meta.top_col,
   686	            meta.rows,
   687	            meta.cols,
   688	            meta.columns
   689	                .iter()
   690	                .map(|c| c.display.as_ref().to_owned())
   691	                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
   692	        )
   693	    };
   694	    if new_rows == 0 || new_cols == 0 {
   695	        return Err(ReplayError::TableResizeRejected {
   696	            index,
   697	            name: name.to_owned(),
   698	            reason: "table rows and cols must both be > 0",
   699	        });
   700	    }
   701	    let added_len = added_columns.len() as u32;
   702	    let removed_len = removed_columns.len() as u32;
   703	    if removed_len > old_cols {
   704	        return Err(ReplayError::TableResizeRejected {
   705	            index,
   706	            name: name.to_owned(),
   707	            reason: "removed_columns count exceeds existing column count",
   708	        });
   709	    }
   710	    // Arithmetic invariant.
   711	    if new_cols != old_cols + added_len - removed_len {
   712	        return Err(ReplayError::TableResizeRejected {
   713	            index,
   714	            name: name.to_owned(),
   715	            reason: "new_cols does not match old_cols + added - removed",
   716	        });
   717	    }
   718	    // removed_columns must exactly match trailing displays
   719	    // (case-insensitive).
   720	    let trailing_start = (old_cols - removed_len) as usize;
   721	    for (i, expected) in removed_columns.iter().enumerate() {
   722	        let idx = trailing_start + i;
   723	        if !old_displays[idx].eq_ignore_ascii_case(expected) {
   724	            return Err(ReplayError::TableResizeRejected {
   725	                index,
   726	                name: name.to_owned(),
   727	                reason: "removed_columns do not match trailing columns",
   728	            });
   729	        }
   730	    }
   731	    // added_columns: non-empty + unique vs final roster.
   732	    if added_columns.iter().any(|s| s.is_empty()) {
   733	        return Err(ReplayError::TableResizeRejected {
   734	            index,
   735	            name: name.to_owned(),
   736	            reason: "added column names cannot be empty",
   737	        });
   738	    }
   739	    // Build the final canonical (lowercase) roster.
   740	    let mut final_canon: Vec<String> = old_displays
   741	        .iter()
   742	        .take(trailing_start)
   743	        .map(|d| d.to_ascii_lowercase())
   744	        .collect();
   745	    for a in added_columns {
   746	        final_canon.push(a.to_ascii_lowercase());
   747	    }
   748	    {
   749	        use std::collections::HashSet;
   750	        let mut seen: HashSet<&str> = HashSet::new();
   751	        for cn in &final_canon {
   752	            if !seen.insert(cn.as_str()) {
   753	                return Err(ReplayError::TableResizeRejected {
   754	                    index,
   755	                    name: name.to_owned(),
   756	                    reason: "final column roster has duplicate canonical names",
   757	                });
   758	            }
   759	        }
   760	    }
   761	    // Footprint-overlap check: only cells NEWLY claimed by this resize
   762	    // need checking. Old-footprint cells already belong to this table.
   763	    // We test all cells in [top_row..top_row+new_rows] x [top_col..
   764	    // top_col+new_cols] and skip those inside the OLD footprint.
   765	    let old_end_row = top_row + old_rows;
   766	    let old_end_col = top_col + old_cols;
   767	    let new_end_row = top_row + new_rows;
   768	    let new_end_col = top_col + new_cols;
   769	    for r in top_row..new_end_row {
   770	        for c in top_col..new_end_col {
   771	            let inside_old = r < old_end_row && c < old_end_col;
   772	            if inside_old {
   773	                continue;
   774	            }
   775	            if let Some(other) = workbook.table_at(sheet, r, c) {
   776	                if !other.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&canonical) {
   777	                    return Err(ReplayError::TableResizeRejected {
   778	                        index,
   779	                        name: name.to_owned(),
   780	                        reason: "new footprint overlaps an existing table",
   781	                    });
   782	                }
   783	            }
   784	            // **W5-124 (Phase 4.8.J.2):** spill-anchor check mirrors
   785	            // producer side. Per design § 4.3 invariant #5, tables
   786	            // block ALL spill anchors inside their footprint.
   787	            if workbook.spill_anchor_at(sheet, r, c).is_some() {
   788	                return Err(ReplayError::TableResizeRejected {
   789	                    index,
   790	                    name: name.to_owned(),
   791	                    reason: "new footprint contains a spill anchor",
   792	                });
   793	            }
   794	        }
   795	    }
   796	    // ----- Mutation -----
   797	    // Allocate new column ids BEFORE taking &mut on the table entry
   798	    // (allocate_column_id lives on TableTable not TableMetadata).
   799	    let new_ids: Vec<u32> = (0..added_columns.len())
   800	        .map(|_| workbook.tables_mut().allocate_column_id())
   801	        .collect();
   802	    let meta = workbook
   803	        .tables_mut()
   804	        .get_mut(&canonical)
   805	        .expect("verified at top");
   806	    meta.rows = new_rows;
   807	    meta.cols = new_cols;
   808	    meta.columns
   809	        .truncate(meta.columns.len() - removed_len as usize);
   810	    for (cn, id) in added_columns.iter().zip(new_ids) {
   811	        meta.columns.push(TableColumn {
   812	            id,
   813	            name: std::sync::Arc::from(cn.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str()),
   814	            display: std::sync::Arc::from(cn.as_str()),
   815	            totals_function: None,
   816	        });
   817	    }
   818	    workbook.tables_mut().bump_generation();
   819	    Ok(())
   820	}
   821	
   822	/// **W5-118 (Phase 4.8.H):** apply a `CreateTable` op against the
   823	/// workbook. Validates the same invariants the producer side checks
   824	/// (mirror via `WorkbookRuntime::create_table`).
   825	#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
   826	fn apply_create_table(
   827	    workbook: &mut Workbook,
   828	    index: usize,
   829	    name: &str,
   830	    sheet: SheetId,
   831	    top_row: RowId,
   832	    top_col: ColId,
   833	    rows: u32,
   834	    cols: u32,
   835	    has_header: bool,
   836	    has_totals: bool,
   837	    column_names: &[String],
   838	) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
   839	    use ql_storage::{TableColumn, TableMetadata};
   840	    let canonical: std::sync::Arc<str> = std::sync::Arc::from(name.to_ascii_uppercase().as_str());
   841	    if workbook.tables().lookup(&canonical).is_some() {
   842	        return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   843	            index,
   844	            name: name.to_owned(),
   845	            reason: "table with this canonical name already exists",
   846	        });
   847	    }
   848	    if workbook.names().lookup_ci(&canonical).is_some() {
   849	        return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   850	            index,
   851	            name: name.to_owned(),
   852	            reason: "defined-name with this canonical name already exists (shared namespace)",
   853	        });
   854	    }
   855	    if column_names.len() != cols as usize {
   856	        return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   857	            index,
   858	            name: name.to_owned(),
   859	            reason: "column_names length does not match cols",
   860	        });
   861	    }
   862	    if column_names.iter().any(|s| s.is_empty()) {
   863	        return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   864	            index,
   865	            name: name.to_owned(),
   866	            reason: "table column names cannot be empty",
   867	        });
   868	    }
   869	    // Column-name uniqueness (case-insensitive).
   870	    {
   871	        use std::collections::HashSet;
   872	        let mut seen: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();
   873	        for cn in column_names {
   874	            if !seen.insert(cn.to_ascii_lowercase()) {
   875	                return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   876	                    index,
   877	                    name: name.to_owned(),
   878	                    reason: "table column names must be unique (case-insensitive)",
   879	                });
   880	            }
   881	        }
   882	    }
   883	    if workbook.sheet(sheet).is_none() {
   884	        return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   885	            index,
   886	            name: name.to_owned(),
   887	            reason: "table references unknown sheet",
   888	        });
   889	    }
   890	    if rows == 0 || cols == 0 {
   891	        return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   892	            index,
   893	            name: name.to_owned(),
   894	            reason: "table rows and cols must both be > 0",
   895	        });
   896	    }
   897	    // Non-overlap + no-spill-anchor inside the footprint.
   898	    // **W5-124 (Phase 4.8.J.2):** spill-anchor check mirrors producer
   899	    // side per design § 4.3 invariant #5.
   900	    for r in top_row..top_row + rows {
   901	        for c in top_col..top_col + cols {
   902	            if workbook.table_at(sheet, r, c).is_some() {
   903	                return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   904	                    index,
   905	                    name: name.to_owned(),
   906	                    reason: "table footprint overlaps an existing table",
   907	                });
   908	            }
   909	            if workbook.spill_anchor_at(sheet, r, c).is_some() {
   910	                return Err(ReplayError::TableCreateRejected {
   911	                    index,
   912	                    name: name.to_owned(),
   913	                    reason: "table footprint contains a spill anchor",
   914	                });
   915	            }
   916	        }
   917	    }
   918	    // Build columns with freshly-allocated ids.
   919	    let columns: Vec<TableColumn> = column_names
   920	        .iter()
   921	        .map(|cn| TableColumn {
   922	            id: workbook.tables_mut().allocate_column_id(),
   923	            name: std::sync::Arc::from(cn.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str()),
   924	            display: std::sync::Arc::from(cn.as_str()),
   925	            totals_function: None,
   926	        })
   927	        .collect();
   928	    let meta = TableMetadata {
   929	        name: canonical.clone(),
   930	        display_name: std::sync::Arc::from(name),
   931	        sheet,
   932	        top_row,
   933	        top_col,
   934	        rows,
   935	        cols,
   936	        has_header,
   937	        has_totals,
   938	        columns,
   939	    };
   940	    workbook.tables_mut().insert(canonical, meta);
   941	    Ok(())
   942	}
   943	
   944	fn validate_cell(
   945	    workbook: &Workbook,
   946	    sheet: SheetId,
   947	    row: RowId,
   948	    col: ColId,
   949	    index: usize,
   950	) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
   951	    let count = workbook.sheet_count();
   952	    if (sheet as usize) >= count {
   953	        return Err(ReplayError::InvalidSheet {
   954	            index,

  1910	    /// - `removed_columns` exactly match trailing columns (case-insensitive).
  1911	    /// - `added_columns`: non-empty entries; final roster has unique
  1912	    ///   canonical (lowercase) names.
  1913	    /// - New footprint cells outside the OLD footprint don't overlap
  1914	    ///   another table.
  1915	    ///
  1916	    /// NOTE: a spill-anchor check inside the new footprint is
  1917	    /// intentionally OMITTED to mirror `create_table`, which doesn't
  1918	    /// check either. The runtime invariant § 4.3 #5 is enforced at
  1919	    /// `write_spill` time today; closing this uniformly across
  1920	    /// create+resize is a separate follow-up.
  1921	    ///
  1922	    /// No formula-text rewriting: resize doesn't change column NAMES
  1923	    /// of surviving columns, so existing references stay valid.
  1924	    /// Re-binding picks up the new range/columns on next eval via the
  1925	    /// cleared plan cache.
  1926	    ///
  1927	    /// **Caller responsibility:** resize bumps `TableTable::generation`
  1928	    /// and clears the plan cache, but does NOT dirty individual
  1929	    /// formula cells. Cached SUM values etc. from BEFORE the resize
  1930	    /// remain materialized in the COMPUTED overlay until the caller
  1931	    /// re-triggers eval (typically via [`Self::recompute_all`]).
  1932	    /// Targeted dirty-propagation via a `table_to_formulas` reverse
  1933	    /// index is design § 4.5 / sub-phase 4.8.G.3 (deferred).
  1934	    pub fn resize_table(
  1935	        &mut self,
  1936	        name: &str,
  1937	        new_rows: u32,
  1938	        new_cols: u32,
  1939	        added_columns: Vec<String>,
  1940	        removed_columns: Vec<String>,
  1941	    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
  1942	        use ql_storage::TableColumn;
  1943	        let canonical = name.to_ascii_uppercase();
  1944	        // Snapshot the immutable bits we need to validate.
  1945	        let (sheet, top_row, top_col, old_rows, old_cols, old_displays) = {
  1946	            let meta = self
  1947	                .workbook
  1948	                .tables()
  1949	                .lookup(&canonical)
  1950	                .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::TableNotFound(name.to_owned()))?;
  1951	            (
  1952	                meta.sheet,
  1953	                meta.top_row,
  1954	                meta.top_col,
  1955	                meta.rows,
  1956	                meta.cols,
  1957	                meta.columns
  1958	                    .iter()
  1959	                    .map(|c| c.display.as_ref().to_owned())
  1960	                    .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
  1961	            )
  1962	        };
  1963	        if new_rows == 0 || new_cols == 0 {
  1964	            return Err(RuntimeError::TableResizeRejected {
  1965	                name: name.to_owned(),
  1966	                reason: "table rows and cols must both be > 0",
  1967	            });
  1968	        }
  1969	        let added_len = added_columns.len() as u32;
  1970	        let removed_len = removed_columns.len() as u32;
  1971	        if removed_len > old_cols {
  1972	            return Err(RuntimeError::TableResizeRejected {
  1973	                name: name.to_owned(),
  1974	                reason: "removed_columns count exceeds existing column count",
  1975	            });
  1976	        }
  1977	        if new_cols != old_cols + added_len - removed_len {
  1978	            return Err(RuntimeError::TableResizeRejected {
  1979	                name: name.to_owned(),
  1980	                reason: "new_cols does not match old_cols + added - removed",
  1981	            });
  1982	        }
  1983	        // removed_columns must exactly match trailing displays
  1984	        // (case-insensitive).
  1985	        let trailing_start = (old_cols - removed_len) as usize;
  1986	        for (i, expected) in removed_columns.iter().enumerate() {
  1987	            let idx = trailing_start + i;
  1988	            if !old_displays[idx].eq_ignore_ascii_case(expected) {
  1989	                return Err(RuntimeError::TableResizeRejected {
  1990	                    name: name.to_owned(),
  1991	                    reason: "removed_columns do not match trailing columns",
  1992	                });
  1993	            }
  1994	        }
  1995	        if added_columns.iter().any(|s| s.is_empty()) {
  1996	            return Err(RuntimeError::TableResizeRejected {
  1997	                name: name.to_owned(),
  1998	                reason: "added column names cannot be empty",
  1999	            });
  2000	        }
  2001	        // Build the final canonical (lowercase) roster + check
  2002	        // uniqueness.
  2003	        let mut final_canon: Vec<String> = old_displays
  2004	            .iter()
  2005	            .take(trailing_start)
  2006	            .map(|d| d.to_ascii_lowercase())
  2007	            .collect();
  2008	        for a in &added_columns {
  2009	            final_canon.push(a.to_ascii_lowercase());
  2010	        }
  2011	        {
  2012	            use std::collections::HashSet;
  2013	            let mut seen: HashSet<&str> = HashSet::new();
  2014	            for cn in &final_canon {
  2015	                if !seen.insert(cn.as_str()) {
  2016	                    return Err(RuntimeError::TableResizeRejected {
  2017	                        name: name.to_owned(),
  2018	                        reason: "final column roster has duplicate canonical names",
  2019	                    });
  2020	                }
  2021	            }
  2022	        }
  2023	        // Footprint-overlap + no-spill-anchor checks: only cells NEWLY
  2024	        // claimed need checking (cells in the OLD footprint already
  2025	        // belong to this table; create_table verified them at creation
  2026	        // time).
  2027	        // **W5-124 (Phase 4.8.J.2):** spill-anchor check mirrors
  2028	        // create_table's, restricted to newly-claimed cells.
  2029	        let old_end_row = top_row + old_rows;
  2030	        let old_end_col = top_col + old_cols;
  2031	        let new_end_row = top_row + new_rows;
  2032	        let new_end_col = top_col + new_cols;
  2033	        for r in top_row..new_end_row {
  2034	            for c in top_col..new_end_col {
  2035	                let inside_old = r < old_end_row && c < old_end_col;
  2036	                if inside_old {
  2037	                    continue;
  2038	                }
  2039	                if let Some(other) = self.workbook.table_at(sheet, r, c) {
  2040	                    if !other.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&canonical) {
  2041	                        return Err(RuntimeError::TableResizeRejected {
  2042	                            name: name.to_owned(),
  2043	                            reason: "new footprint overlaps an existing table",
  2044	                        });
  2045	                    }
  2046	                }
  2047	                if self.workbook.spill_anchor_at(sheet, r, c).is_some() {
  2048	                    return Err(RuntimeError::TableResizeRejected {
  2049	                        name: name.to_owned(),
  2050	                        reason: "new footprint contains a spill anchor",
  2051	                    });
  2052	                }
  2053	            }
  2054	        }
  2055	        // ----- Op-log append (BEFORE mutation per W5-103) -----
  2056	        if let Some(oplog) = self.oplog.as_deref_mut() {
  2057	            oplog.append(Op::ResizeTable {
  2058	                name: canonical.clone(),
  2059	                new_rows,
  2060	                new_cols,
  2061	                added_columns: added_columns.clone(),
  2062	                removed_columns: removed_columns.clone(),
  2063	            })?;
  2064	        }
  2065	        // ----- Mutation -----
  2066	        // Allocate new column ids first; mutable borrows on TableTable
  2067	        // versus TableMetadata conflict otherwise.
  2068	        let new_ids: Vec<u32> = (0..added_columns.len())
  2069	            .map(|_| self.workbook.tables_mut().allocate_column_id())
  2070	            .collect();
  2071	        let meta = self
  2072	            .workbook
  2073	            .tables_mut()
  2074	            .get_mut(&canonical)
  2075	            .expect("verified at top");
  2076	        meta.rows = new_rows;
  2077	        meta.cols = new_cols;
  2078	        meta.columns
  2079	            .truncate(meta.columns.len() - removed_len as usize);
  2080	        for (cn, id) in added_columns.iter().zip(new_ids) {
  2081	            meta.columns.push(TableColumn {
  2082	                id,
  2083	                name: Arc::from(cn.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str()),
  2084	                display: Arc::from(cn.as_str()),
  2085	                totals_function: None,
  2086	            });
  2087	        }
  2088	        self.workbook.tables_mut().bump_generation();
  2089	        // Plan cache clear so formulas re-bind against the new range
  2090	        // / column roster on next eval. Targeted invalidation via
  2091	        // `table_to_formulas` is design § 4.5 / 4.8.G.3 (deferred).
  2092	        self.plan_cache.clear();
  2093	        Ok(())
  2094	    }

   289	    /// **Loader / mutation-internal API.** Insert a table by canonical
   290	    /// uppercase name. The caller is responsible for:
   291	    /// - canonicalizing the key (uppercase) BEFORE calling.
   292	    /// - validating uniqueness, non-overlap, valid Excel-shaped name
   293	    ///   (design § 5.3) — `WorkbookRuntime::create_table` does this in
   294	    ///   4.8.H.
   295	    ///
   296	    /// Bumps the generation counter on success.
   297	    ///
   298	    /// **W5-123 (Phase 4.8.L):** also auto-bumps `next_column_id` past
   299	    /// the highest column id in `meta.columns`. The `.qbook` loader
   300	    /// (Phase 4.8.L persistence) preserves stable column ids verbatim;
   301	    /// without this self-maintenance the runtime allocator could
   302	    /// later hand out an id that collides with one already in storage.
   303	    pub fn insert(&mut self, canonical: Arc<str>, meta: TableMetadata) {
   304	        if let Some(max_id) = meta.columns.iter().map(|c| c.id).max() {
   305	            self.next_column_id = self.next_column_id.max(max_id + 1);
   306	        }
   307	        self.tables.insert(canonical, meta);
   308	        self.generation = self.generation.wrapping_add(1);
   309	    }

   311	    /// Remove a table by canonical name. Bumps generation iff a removal
   312	    /// occurred (idempotent no-op clears don't invalidate caches).
   313	    pub fn remove(&mut self, canonical: &str) -> Option<TableMetadata> {
   314	        let upper = canonical.to_ascii_uppercase();
   315	        let removed = self.tables.remove(upper.as_str());
   316	        if removed.is_some() {
   317	            self.generation = self.generation.wrapping_add(1);
   318	        }
   319	        removed
   320	    }

   322	    /// Allocate and return the next column id. Each call returns a fresh

   325	    /// 4 billion column allocations is unreachable.
   326	    pub fn allocate_column_id(&mut self) -> u32 {
   327	        let id = self.next_column_id;
   328	        self.next_column_id = self.next_column_id.wrapping_add(1);

   458	        &self.tables
   459	    }

   464	    /// emit op-log entries. Direct callers: qbook loader, tests,

   505	    pub fn set_name(&mut self, name: &str, target: NamedTarget) -> Result<(), NameTableError> {

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! `.qbook/` workbook directory format.
     2	//!
     3	//! Per spec Part V §3 + T2-D01 (Round 7 architectural lock). The on-disk shape:
     4	//!
     5	//! ```text
     6	//! my-workbook.qbook/
     7	//! ├── workbook.toml          # envelope: schema_version, name, sheet list, names
     8	//! └── sheets/
     9	//!     ├── 0.jsonl            # sheet 0 cells, one JSON line per non-blank cell
    10	//!     ├── 1.jsonl
    11	//!     └── ...
    12	//! ```
    13	//!
    14	//! - **TOML envelope** (`workbook.toml`): human-readable metadata, sheet manifest,
    15	//!   defined-names table (since v2). Users can edit it by hand if needed.
    16	//! - **JSONL per sheet**: one cell per line, append-friendly, line-oriented (clean
    17	//!   diffs in tools that don't know about the format). Blank cells without formulas
    18	//!   are NOT emitted — the file is a sparse representation.
    19	//! - **Per-sheet partitioning**: opening one sheet doesn't require parsing all
    20	//!   others. Scales to multi-million-cell workbooks at the partition boundary.
    21	//!
    22	//! ## Schema versions
    23	//!
    24	//! - **v1** (Phase 1 W5-6 + W5-9): envelope { schema_version, name, sheets },
    25	//!   CellRecord { row, col, value, formula? }. CellWireValue ∈ {Number, Boolean,
    26	//!   Text, Error}.
    27	//! - **v2** (Phase 2A.8, this revision; megaudit closure for H2/M8-M12):
    28	//!     - Envelope gains `names: Option<NamesSection>` for `NameTable` persistence
    29	//!       (closes M12). v1 files load as `names: None`.
    30	//!     - `CellWireValue::Pending` variant for formula-bearing cells whose saved
    31	//!       value would be `Value::Blank` (closes M11 — was previously encoded as
    32	//!       `Error(#NULL!)` which conflated a real spreadsheet error with a
    33	//!       not-yet-evaluated formula).
    34	//!     - `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` on every envelope / record / wire-value
    35	//!       struct (closes M9 — previously unknown fields silently dropped on read).
    36	//!
    37	//! ## Compatibility policy
    38	//!
    39	//! - **v1 → v2 load**: ACCEPTED. The v2 reader treats a v1 envelope as
    40	//!   `names: None` and converts legacy `Error(#NULL!)` placeholders into Pending
    41	//!   when the cell carries a formula. Existing v1 fixtures continue to load.
    42	//! - **v2 → v1 load**: REJECTED via the existing `UnsupportedSchema` path. No
    43	//!   silent forward-compat per the no-fallbacks rule.
    44	//! - **Within a version**: `deny_unknown_fields` rejects extra fields loudly.
    45	//!   Any future additive change requires an explicit version bump.
    46	//!
    47	//! ## Atomic save (Phase 2A.8 megaudit H2 closure)
    48	//!
    49	//! Save protocol uses a backup-rename sequence so a crash at any moment leaves
    50	//! either the prior workbook or the new one intact at `path` (or recoverable
    51	//! from a `.bak-<random>` sibling):
    52	//!
    53	//! ```text
    54	//! 1. write_workbook_to_dir(wb, name, &temp)     // temp = <path>.tmp-save-<random>
    55	//! 2. if path.exists(): fs::rename(path, &bak)   // bak = <path>.bak-<random>
    56	//! 3. fs::rename(&temp, path)                    // install new
    57	//! 4. fs::remove_dir_all(&bak)                   // best-effort cleanup (logged on failure)
    58	//! ```
    59	//!
    60	//! Invariant: between any two adjacent steps, **at least one** of `path` or
    61	//! `bak` contains a complete valid workbook.
    62	//!
    63	//! `load_workbook` runs `recover_from_crashed_save` first, which detects
    64	//! orphan `.bak-*` siblings and either rolls forward (cleanup if `path` is
    65	//! valid) or rolls back (rename `bak` → `path` if `path` is missing/invalid).
    66	//!
    67	//! ## Deferred to Phase 3+
    68	//! - Computed-overlay separation (CORR-25 deferred).
    69	//! - Cell formatting / number formats / styles.
    70	//! - Sheet-scoped named ranges (`Sheet1!Local`).
    71	
    72	use std::fs;
    73	use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, BufWriter, Write};
    74	use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
    75	
    76	use ql_storage::{NamedTarget, Sheet, Workbook};
    77	use ql_types::{Address, ColId, ErrorValue, Range, RowId, SheetId, Value, MAX_COLUMN, MAX_ROW};
    78	use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
    79	use thiserror::Error;
    80	
    81	/// On-disk schema version. Bumped on incompatible changes.
    82	///
    83	/// - `1` = Phase 1 W5-6 + W5-9: cell values + optional formulas.
    84	/// - `2` = Phase 2A.8 megaudit closure: adds NameTable persistence, explicit
    85	///   `Pending` CellWireValue, `deny_unknown_fields` strictness.
    86	///
    87	/// Loaders accept v1 AND v2 envelopes (v2 reader rewrites v1's `Error(#NULL!)`
    88	/// placeholders into `Pending` when the cell carries a formula). v1 readers
    89	/// (pre-2A.8 binaries, if any exist) refuse v2 via the `UnsupportedSchema`
    90	/// error path.
    91	/// **W5-71 (Phase 4.5.A.2):** bumped to v3. v3 adds an optional
    92	/// `date_system` envelope field (`"1900"` or `"1904"`, default `"1900"`
    93	/// on missing). Loaders accept v1, v2, AND v3 envelopes. A v2 envelope
    94	/// loaded by a v3 reader gets `Workbook::date_system = Excel1900` (the
    95	/// default). A v3 envelope's `date_system` is currently always set on
    96	/// save; old binaries (v2 readers) refuse v3 via `UnsupportedSchema`.
    97	///
    98	/// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** bumped to v4. v4 adds:
    99	///   - top-level `formats: Option<FormatsSection>` — custom format
   100	///     entries (id >= 164). Excel built-in ids 0-163 are NOT persisted;
   101	///     `FormatTable::default()` re-seeds them on load.
   102	///   - per-sheet `format_overlay: Option<Vec<FormatOverlayEntry>>` —
   103	///     sparse `(row, col, id)` tuples binding cells to format ids.
   104	///
   105	/// **W5-92 (Phase 4.6.D):** bumped to v5. v5 adds:
   106	///   - `NamedEntry.scope: Option<u16>` — `None` = workbook-scoped
   107	///     (existing behavior; v1-v4 default), `Some(id)` = sheet-scoped
   108	///     (new). The field is `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = ...)]`
   109	///     so v1-v4 wire payloads continue to deserialize cleanly with
   110	///     `scope = None` on every entry.
   111	///
   112	/// Loaders accept v1..=v5. v1-v4 envelopes load with all-workbook-
   113	/// scoped names (regression-neutral). v4 readers refuse v5 via
   114	/// `UnsupportedSchema` (the schema_version comparison upgrade is the
   115	/// only fail-loud surface — `NamedEntry` itself uses `serde(default)`
   116	/// so a v4 reader that somehow saw a v5 `NamedEntry` with `scope: Some`
   117	/// would deserialize but then mis-route the name to the workbook scope
   118	/// — the version gate prevents that path).
   119	///
   120	/// **W5-123 (Phase 4.8.L):** bumped to v6. v6 adds:
   121	///   - top-level `tables: Option<TablesSection>` — workbook-scoped
   122	///     table metadata (name, footprint, header/totals flags, ordered
   123	///     column roster with stable ids + display + canonical names +
   124	///     optional TotalsFunction). v1-v5 envelopes omit; loader treats
   125	///     `None` as "no tables registered."
   126	///
   127	/// **Compatibility (design § 10.3 + MEDIUM-7 closure):**
   128	///   - v6 reader loading v1-v5: tables field absent → empty TableTable.
   129	///   - v5 reader loading v6: refused via the existing
   130	///     `schema_version > WORKBOOK_SCHEMA_VERSION` check → loud
   131	///     `UnsupportedSchema`. Silently dropping table metadata would
   132	///     break formulas referencing `Sales[Qty]` after re-load (the
   133	///     formula would surface `BindError::UnknownTable` → `#NAME?`
   134	///     where pre-save it was a valid SUM).
   135	///   - v6 reader writing v5-compat: NOT supported in 4.8 (Phase 5+).
   136	pub const WORKBOOK_SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = 6;
   137	
   138	/// The earliest schema version this reader still accepts. v1 fixtures (Phase 1)
   139	/// continue to load on v2 binaries; older versions would need explicit handling.
   140	///
   141	/// Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 LOW-9: now `pub` so external callers (e.g.
   142	/// `ql-oplog`) can inspect the accepted version range without reaching into
   143	/// qbook_format internals.
   144	pub const MIN_SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = 1;
   145	
   146	/// Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 H1: sentinel filename written inside every
   147	/// engine-saved workbook directory. Recovery scanning uses this to
   148	/// distinguish engine-owned `.bak-<random>` siblings (which it may delete
   149	/// or restore) from user-created sibling directories that happen to match
   150	/// the `.bak-*` pattern (e.g., `book.qbook.bak-2025-review`).
   151	///
   152	/// The marker is written LAST in `write_workbook_to_dir` (after envelope +
   153	/// all sheet JSONLs), so a partial write doesn't leave a misleading marker
   154	/// in an incomplete directory. A v1 workbook re-saved on a v2 binary gains
   155	/// the marker after one successful save; until then, recovery will refuse
   156	/// to touch marker-less `.bak-*` siblings.
   157	const ATOMIC_SAVE_MARKER_FILENAME: &str = ".atomic-save-marker-v1";
   158	
   159	/// Errors that can occur during save / load.
   160	#[derive(Debug, Error)]
   161	pub enum QbookError {
   162	    #[error("I/O error: {0}")]
   163	    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),
   164	
   165	    #[error("TOML deserialize error: {0}")]
   166	    TomlDe(#[from] toml::de::Error),
   167	
   168	    #[error("TOML serialize error: {0}")]
   169	    TomlSer(#[from] toml::ser::Error),
   170	
   171	    #[error("JSON error: {0}")]
   172	    Json(#[from] serde_json::Error),
   173	
   174	    #[error(
   175	        "unsupported schema version {found} (this build supports versions \
   176	         {MIN_SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION}..={WORKBOOK_SCHEMA_VERSION})"
   177	    )]
   178	    UnsupportedSchema { found: u32 },
   179	
   180	    /// Phase 2A.8 audit M12: a NamedTargetWire couldn't be reconstructed into a
   181	    /// `ql_storage::NamedTarget`. Carries the name and a reason string.
   182	    #[error("malformed name {name:?}: {reason}")]
   183	    MalformedName { name: String, reason: String },
   184	
   185	    /// Phase 2A.8 audit H2: `path` doesn't have a parent directory or sensible
   186	    /// file name, so the atomic-save temp/backup paths can't be derived.
   187	    /// Surfaces from `make_save_paths` rather than the prior `unwrap_or` silent
   188	    /// fallback.
   189	    #[error("invalid workbook path {path:?}: {reason}")]
   190	    InvalidPath { path: PathBuf, reason: &'static str },
   191	
   192	    /// Phase 2A.13 audit cycle-3 M5: step 3 of the atomic-save protocol
   193	    /// (rename temp → target) failed AND the rollback (rename backup → target)
   194	    /// also failed. The user-visible state is "workbook may now be unavailable
   195	    /// at `path`; backup data may still be at `backup_path`." Surfaces this
   196	    /// fact directly instead of just bubbling up the original temp-rename
   197	    /// error and burying the rollback failure in stderr.
   198	    #[error(
   199	        "atomic save rollback failed at {backup_path:?}: install error ({install}); \
   200	         backup restore error ({restore}). Workbook may be unavailable at the target; \
   201	         the backup directory still holds the prior state."
   202	    )]
   203	    AtomicSaveRollbackFailed {
   204	        backup_path: PathBuf,
   205	        install: String,
   206	        restore: String,
   207	    },
   208	
   209	    #[error("malformed cell record in {file:?} at line {line}: {detail}")]
   210	    MalformedCell {
   211	        file: PathBuf,
   212	        line: usize,
   213	        detail: String,
   214	    },
   215	
   216	    #[error("workbook directory {path:?} does not exist or is not a directory")]
   217	    NotADirectory { path: PathBuf },
   218	
   219	    /// A cell value that doesn't survive JSON round-trip cleanly. serde_json
   220	    /// silently encodes NaN/Inf as `null`, which then fails to load as a Number.
   221	    /// Save-side validation rejects them with this error. Audit M6 fix (2026-05-12).
   222	    #[error(
   223	        "non-finite f64 cell at sheet {sheet} row {row} col {col}: {value} cannot be serialized"
   224	    )]
   225	    NonFiniteNumber {
   226	        sheet: SheetId,
   227	        row: RowId,
   228	        col: ColId,
   229	        value: f64,
   230	    },
   231	
   232	    #[error("missing required file {file:?}")]
   233	    MissingFile { file: PathBuf },
   234	
   235	    /// Sheet ids in the envelope must be sequential 0..N. Audit M3 fix (2026-05-12).
   236	    #[error("non-sequential sheet ids in envelope: expected id {expected}, found {found}")]
   237	    NonSequentialSheetIds { expected: u16, found: u16 },
   238	
   239	    /// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** envelope's `formats` section
   240	    /// references an id whose registration collides with the
   241	    /// pre-populated built-in table OR an internal inconsistency
   242	    /// (same string at two ids). The `details` field is the
   243	    /// `FormatTableError`'s `Debug` rendering.
   244	    #[error("malformed format entry id={id}: {details}")]
   245	    MalformedFormat { id: u32, details: String },
   246	
   247	    /// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** per-sheet `format_overlay` carries
   248	    /// coordinates outside the workbook's row/col bounds.
   249	    #[error("malformed format overlay entry on sheet {sheet}: row={row}, col={col} ({why})")]
   250	    MalformedFormatOverlay {
   251	        sheet: u16,
   252	        row: u32,
   253	        col: u32,
   254	        why: &'static str,
   255	    },
   256	
   257	    /// **W5-93 (Phase 4.6.E closure):** sheet name in the envelope's
   258	    /// `[[sheets]]` section failed `Workbook::validate_sheet_name`
   259	    /// (empty, duplicate under canonical comparison, or contains an
   260	    /// Excel-reserved character `: \ / ? * [ ]`). Codex HIGH-1: the
   261	    /// loader previously accepted any name silently, so a hand-edited
   262	    /// `.qbook` could enter storage with conflicting sheets.
   263	    #[error("malformed sheet {sheet_id}: name {name:?} ({source})")]
   264	    MalformedSheet {
   265	        sheet_id: u16,
   266	        name: String,
   267	        #[source]
   268	        source: ql_storage::SheetNameError,
   269	    },
   270	
   271	    /// **W5-123 (Phase 4.8.L):** entry in the envelope's `[[tables]]`
   272	    /// section failed a structural invariant — unknown sheet id, zero
   273	    /// dims, columns length / cols mismatch, or any other shape error
   274	    /// that would leave `TableTable` inconsistent. A hand-edited or
   275	    /// corrupted v6 envelope surfaces here loudly rather than silently
   276	    /// producing a table that fails to bind formulas downstream.
   277	    #[error("malformed table {name:?}: {reason}")]
   278	    MalformedTable { name: String, reason: String },
   279	}

   281	/// TOML envelope for the workbook. Top-level metadata.
   282	///
   283	/// Phase 2A.8: `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` rejects any unknown TOML key —
   284	/// previously the loader silently dropped extras, which would let a v2-only
   285	/// field be skipped by a still-v1 reader. Any future field requires an
   286	/// explicit schema-version bump.
   287	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   288	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   289	pub struct WorkbookEnvelope {
   290	    pub schema_version: u32,
   291	    /// User-supplied workbook name. Defaults to the directory name when omitted.
   292	    pub name: String,
   293	    /// Per-sheet metadata in id order.
   294	    pub sheets: Vec<SheetEnvelope>,
   295	    /// Phase 2A.8: workbook-scope defined-names section. Present in v2 envelopes
   296	    /// (`Some` even when empty), absent in v1. `#[serde(default)]` lets v1 files
   297	    /// load with `names: None`; the loader treats that as "no names registered."
   298	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   299	    pub names: Option<NamesSection>,
   300	    /// **W5-71 (Phase 4.5.A.2):** Excel date system. Present in v3 envelopes
   301	    /// (always `Some` on save), absent in v1/v2 (loader maps `None` to
   302	    /// `DateSystem::Excel1900` — the default).
   303	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   304	    pub date_system: Option<DateSystemWire>,
   305	    /// **W5-81 (Phase 4.5.D part 5):** workbook-level format-string
   306	    /// interning table. v4 envelopes carry custom format entries (id ≥
   307	    /// 164) only; built-ins 0-163 are re-seeded by `FormatTable::default()`
   308	    /// on load. v1-v3 envelopes omit the field; the loader treats `None`
   309	    /// as "no custom formats registered" and proceeds with defaults.
   310	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   311	    pub formats: Option<FormatsSection>,
   312	    /// **W5-123 (Phase 4.8.L):** workbook-scoped table metadata. v6
   313	    /// envelopes carry the section when at least one table is
   314	    /// registered; v1-v5 omit (and v6 omits an empty `TableTable` to
   315	    /// keep TOML minimal). Loader treats `None` as "no tables
   316	    /// registered."
   317	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   318	    pub tables: Option<TablesSection>,
   319	}
   320	
   321	/// **W5-71 (Phase 4.5.A.2):** wire representation of `ql_types::DateSystem`.
   322	/// Serialized as a string `"1900"` or `"1904"` for human-readable TOML.
   323	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   324	pub enum DateSystemWire {
   325	    #[serde(rename = "1900")]
   326	    Excel1900,
   327	    #[serde(rename = "1904")]
   328	    Excel1904,
   329	}
   330	
   331	impl DateSystemWire {
   332	    pub fn from_runtime(system: ql_types::DateSystem) -> Self {
   333	        match system {
   334	            ql_types::DateSystem::Excel1900 => Self::Excel1900,
   335	            ql_types::DateSystem::Excel1904 => Self::Excel1904,
   336	        }
   337	    }
   338	
   339	    pub fn to_runtime(self) -> ql_types::DateSystem {
   340	        match self {
   341	            Self::Excel1900 => ql_types::DateSystem::Excel1900,
   342	            Self::Excel1904 => ql_types::DateSystem::Excel1904,
   343	        }
   344	    }
   345	}

   347	#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   348	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
   349	pub struct SheetEnvelope {
   350	    pub id: u16,
   351	    pub name: String,
   352	    /// Per-sheet chunk size (default 16384). Phase 1 W5-6 captures the constructed
   353	    /// value so loaders reconstruct sheets at the same chunk layout.
   354	    pub chunk_rows: u32,
   355	    /// Highest-row written + 1, conservative. Reads beyond this still return Blank;
   356	    /// the field exists for save-side allocation hints and observability.
   357	    pub row_extent: u32,

   605	    /// Decode the wire value into a `ql_types::Value`. `Pending` decodes to
   606	    /// `Value::Blank` — the cell is in a transient state until recompute runs.
   607	    pub fn to_value(&self) -> Result<Value, QbookError> {
   608	        match self {
   609	            CellWireValue::Number(n) => Ok(Value::number(*n)),
   610	            CellWireValue::Boolean(b) => Ok(Value::Boolean(*b)),
   611	            CellWireValue::Text(s) => Ok(Value::text(s.as_str())),

   613	                Some(e) => Ok(Value::Error(e)),

   615	                    file: PathBuf::new(),
   616	                    line: 0,
   617	                    detail: format!("unknown error variant: {s:?}"),
   618	                }),
   619	            },
   620	            CellWireValue::Pending => Ok(Value::Blank),
   621	        }
   622	    }

   624	    /// True iff this is the Phase 2A.8 Pending sentinel (formula-bearing cell
   625	    /// with no evaluated value).
   626	    pub fn is_pending(&self) -> bool {
   627	        matches!(self, CellWireValue::Pending)
   628	    }
